For i from N down to 1: we need to place i at the positions[i-1]-th empty slot from left. Because when inserting from back, the relative order of earlier inserted elements remains. So we can maintain BIT over empty slots. Initially all slots empty (value 1). For each i from N down to 1: we find the index idx such that prefix sum up to idx equals positions[i-1].
